

USE [TrxSQL]

/****** Объект:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS1_0]    Дата создания скрипта: 27.11.2026 11:19:02 ******/ 
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS1_0]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int =0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @KolDolg int
DECLARE @tmp int
DECLARE @IsZakup bit
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end
SET @IsZakup = 0
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	if @Otv = 0 begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv = @Otv 
	end
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
	--ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
	if @f = '9909041763' begin
		SELECT @IsZakup = case when COUNT(*)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 
		FROM dbo.SubPermissions with (nolock)
		WHERE IDPerm = 'MoveEl4Stat' and usr = CURRENT_USER 
	end
end
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	SET @KolDolg = -2147483648
end
else begin
	SET @KolDolg = 0
end
CREATE TABLE #TmpEls (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY)
if @Rgm = 1 begin
	insert into #TmpEls (SkladID )
	select qryfrmSt.Код 
	FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own  as qryfrmSt
	--inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON qryfrmSt.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
	WHERE 
	(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
	OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
	and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
	and
	(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.f1 =0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.f1 - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
				CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING( ((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/case when NU = 0 Then 1 else NU end) ) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
				END
			END
		END
	END  >@KolDolg)
	and qryfrmSt.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
end
if @Rgm = 2 begin
	insert into #TmpEls (SkladID)
	select qryfrmSt.Код 
	FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav  as qryfrmSt
	WHERE
	(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
	OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
	and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
	and
	(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
				CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/case when NU = 0 Then 1 else NU end)) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
				END
			END
		END
	END>0) 
	and qryfrmSt.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
end
if @Rgm = 3 begin
	insert into #TmpEls (SkladID)
	select qryfrmSt.Код 
	FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0  as qryfrmSt
	WHERE 
	(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
	OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
	and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
	and
	(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolRes4OverKol =0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolRes4OverKol - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
				CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/case when NU = 0 Then 1 else NU end)) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
				END
			END
		END
	END > @KolDolg )
	and qryfrmSt.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
end
CREATE TABLE #TmpAvt (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Avt tinyint)
--and @IsZakup = 0 для ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpAvt  (SkladID,Avt)
	SELECT DstID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblMoveEl with (nolock)
	inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qSel.SkladID 
	--inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
	--inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
	FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
	WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0
	GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qRes.SkladID 
	
	WHERE dbo.tblMoveEl.Repl < 3
end
else begin
	if @SkladID =0 begin
		if @FZ275 = 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt  (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qSel.SkladID
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579 and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >= 0
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ = 1
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.ParentID >=0 
		end
		if @FZ275 = -1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) 
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0 
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qRes 
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275 > 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID =@FZ275 
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes 
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
	end 
	else begin
		if @SkladID =-500 begin
			SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
			Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,0 as Долг,
			0,'' as FirstName,0 as ProblKol,
			'' as UchetID,0 as FZ275Kol,
			'' as SharpName,0 as NotRec,'' as NmeCat,
			'' as [CreatorCode],'' as Примечание,0 as [Активный],
			0 as FullDolg,'0' as Analog ,0 as IDInvoice ,
			VP5,NoAnalog,'' as WhatFirmTxt,0 as ExistMove,
			0 as SostProblEl,'' as SrcProblEl
			FROM  dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK)  WHERE Код = -500
			RETURN
			--=IIf([Недополучено]-[Долг]-[TS]<0;[Долг]-[Недополучено]+[TS];0)
		end
		else begin
		if @FZ275=0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qSel.SkladID
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID 
		end
		if @FZ275=-1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) 
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
		end
		if @FZ275>0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) 
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID = @FZ275 
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes 
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			 --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		end
	end
end
--SET @EDta = GETDATE()
--SET @BDta = DATEADD(YEAR,-1,@EDta)
if @Rgm=1 begin
--DELETE FROM #TmpAvt 
--FROM #TmpAvt as q
--left outer join 
--(select qryfrmSt.Код 
--FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own  as qryfrmSt
--inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON qryfrmSt.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
--INNER JOIN 
--(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #tmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
--WHERE 
--(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
-- OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
--and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
--and
--(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.f1 =0 THEN 0 ELSE
--	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.f1 - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
--		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
--			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
--			--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
--			case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
--			else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
--			END
--		END
--	END
--END  >@KolDolg)) as qSel ON q.SkladID = qSel.Код 
--WHERE qSel.Код is null

SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQLNew([Код],qryfrmSt.Недополучено+ qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak) END as IsDelayed,
0 as IsDelayed,
UchetID,
--,0 as IsBoth,
CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,
SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0) as OrderKat,
--по письму Кулева от 29.11.19
CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END as TS,
--isnull(qryTS.KolPok,0) as KolPok,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog,qryfrmSt.Avt , FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],Примечание,[Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,WhatFirmTxt ,
CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as WF,
qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,
--как поле NeedEls карточки элемента (21.01.21)
cast(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
			END
		END
	END
END as int) as txtSV,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryFrmSt.DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
qryfrmSt.RequestSch,qryfrmSt.MainPlace,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk

--,CURRENT_USER 
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak , Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,
CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [KolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
--'-1        0         '  as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,NU,flgNU,f1 as KolInRes,TS,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,qrySklad.Avt ,
RequestSch,MainPlace

FROM #tmpAvt as qrySklad
INNER JOIN dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own  ON qrySklad.SkladID = dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Код 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
) as qryfrmSt


LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,qLst.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
inner join #TmpAvt as qLst ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qLst.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, qLst.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON qryfrmSt.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)

ORDER BY 
--isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , 
qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
0 as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,NotRec,NmeCat,0 as OrderKat,0 as TS,0 as KolPok,VP5
,NoAnalog,qryfrmSt.Avt,FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],
Примечание, [Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,
--как поле NeedEls карточки элемента (21.01.21)
cast(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
			--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
			case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
			else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
			END
		END
	END
END as int) as txtSV,qryfrmSt.UpKoef, 0 as DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,qryfrmSt.RequestSch,qryfrmSt.MainPlace,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak ,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,NU,flgNU,qrySklad.Avt,
VP5,NoAnalog,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [KolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
--'-1        0         '  as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SrcProblEl,KolInRes,0 as TS,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.UpKoef,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.MinToBuy,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.RequestSch,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.MainPlace
FROM  #TmpAvt as qrySklad
inner join dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav on qrySklad.SkladID = dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
) as qryfrmSt
left outer join
(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,qLst.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
inner join #TmpAvt as qLst ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qLst.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, qLst.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON qryfrmSt.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
ORDER BY isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end

Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,

	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
			--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
			case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
			else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
			END
		END
	END
END as int) as txtSV,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,RequestSch,MainPlace,

FROM

Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak , Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,

FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice,

--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [Количество]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
'-1        0         ' as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SrcProblEl,NU,flgNU,KolRes4OverKol as KolInRes,TS,UPKoef,MinToBuy ,DfltIsp,

) as qryfrmSt

WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID

(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,qLst.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
inner join #TmpAvt as qLst ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qLst.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, qLst.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q

ORDER BY isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS8]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int =0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @BDta datetime
DECLARE @EDta datetime
DECLARE @KolDolg int
DECLARE @Who varchar(20)
declare @tmp int
DECLARE @IsZakup bit
SET @IsZakup = 0 
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
	--ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
	if @f = '9909041763' begin
		SELECT @IsZakup = case when COUNT(*)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 
		FROM dbo.SubPermissions with (nolock)
		WHERE IDPerm = 'MoveEl4Stat' and usr = CURRENT_USER 
	end
end
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

SET @Who = CURRENT_USER 
CREATE TABLE #TmpAvt (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Avt tinyint)
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	SET @KolDolg = -2147483648
	INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
	SELECT DstID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblMoveEl with (nolock)
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
	WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0
	GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qRes.SkladID 
	
	WHERE Repl < 3
end
else begin
	SET @KolDolg = 0
	if @SkladID =0 begin
		if @FZ275 = 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579 and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >= 0
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ = 1
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.ParentID >=0 
		end
		if @FZ275 = -1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275 > 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID =@FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
	end 
	else begin
		if @SkladID =-500 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
			VALUES (-500,0,@Who )
		end
		else begin
		if @FZ275=0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID 
		end
		if @FZ275=-1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275>0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID = @FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		end
	end
end
SET @EDta = GETDATE()
SET @BDta = DATEADD(YEAR,-1,@EDta)
if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,

case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0) as OrderKat,isnull(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--qryTS.KolPok ,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog ,qrySklad.Avt ,

FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS8.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,

FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St 
FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS8
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS8.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS8.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
	(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
	--отключён по письму Знака от 16.05.19
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch

group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND 
--([FullDolg] + ISNULL(qryTS.TS,0) >@KolDolg )
ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin

FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,

FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav

WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 

and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl = 255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 
WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND ([Долг]>@KolDolg )

ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end

dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,

FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,

 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and  (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl = 255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt 
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID
--LEFT OUTER JOIN
--(SELECT dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID, 
--CASE WHEN dbo.tblTehStock.kol is null THEN CASE WHEN ISNULL(qryPok.KolPok,0)>2 THEN isnull(qryKat.KolInKat ,5000) ELSE CASE WHEN isnull(qryKat.KolInKat ,5000)/10>500 THEN isnull(qryKat.KolInKat ,5000)/10 ELSE 500 END END ELSE dbo.tblTehStock.kol END as TS,
--qryKat.KolInKat ,qryPok.KolPok 
--FROM dbo.tblTehStock WITH (NOLOCK) 
--LEFT OUTER JOIN
--(SELECT COUNT(*) as KolPok,SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество ) as Kupleno, dbo.tblAll.SkladID  FROM dbo.tblShceta WITH (NOLOCK)
--INNER JOIN dbo.tblAll WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblShceta.ShcID = dbo.tblAll.ShcID 
--WHERE ДатаНакладной BETWEEN @BDta AND @EDta
--GROUP BY dbo.tblAll.SkladID) as qryPok ON dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID = qryPok.SkladID

--FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)

--(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
--WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
--GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
--GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID = qryKat.SkladID) as qryTS ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID

FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)

WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER

(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND

end

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS8_0]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int =0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @BDta datetime
DECLARE @EDta datetime
DECLARE @KolDolg int
DECLARE @Who varchar(20)
declare @tmp int
DECLARE @IsZakup bit
SET @IsZakup = 0 
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) 
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
	--ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
	if @f = '9909041763' begin
		SELECT @IsZakup = case when COUNT(*)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 
		FROM dbo.SubPermissions with (nolock)
		WHERE IDPerm = 'MoveEl4Stat' and usr = CURRENT_USER 
	end
end
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

SET @Who = CURRENT_USER 
CREATE TABLE #TmpAvt (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Avt tinyint)
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	SET @KolDolg = -2147483648
	INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
	SELECT DstID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblMoveEl with (nolock)
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
	WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0
	GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qRes.SkladID 
	
	WHERE Repl < 3
end
else begin
	SET @KolDolg = 0
	if @SkladID =0 begin
		if @FZ275 = 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579 and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >= 0
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ = 1
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.ParentID >=0 
		end
		if @FZ275 = -1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275 > 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID =@FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
	end 
	else begin
		if @SkladID =-500 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
			VALUES (-500,0,@Who )
		end
		else begin
		if @FZ275=0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID 
		end
		if @FZ275=-1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275>0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID = @FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		end
	end
end
SET @EDta = GETDATE()
SET @BDta = DATEADD(YEAR,-1,@EDta)
if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг, Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0) as OrderKat,isnull(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--qryTS.KolPok ,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog ,qrySklad.Avt ,
[CreatorCode], qryfrmSt.ExistMove,qryfrmSt.FZ275Kol,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.MinToBuy,qryfrmSt.DfltIsp,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS8.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St 
FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS8
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS8.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS8.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
inner JOIN 
	(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND 
--([FullDolg] + ISNULL(qryTS.TS,0) >@KolDolg )
ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,

Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,

FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 

and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl = 255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
inner JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND ([Долг]>@KolDolg )

ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end

case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0)  as OrderKat,ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--qryTS.KolPok ,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog,qrySklad.Avt ,
[CreatorCode],qryfrmSt.ExistMove,qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.FZ275Kol,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.MinToBuy,qryfrmSt.DfltIsp,

FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St 
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and  (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl = 255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt 
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND 
--(qryfrmSt.FullDolg-qryfrmSt.Недополучено + CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Элемент='Давальческое сырье' THEN 0 ELSE ISNULL(qryTS.TS,0) END >@KolDolg )

ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS9_0]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int =1,@WhatFirm smallint =0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm и далее оставлены для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
if @SkladID = -500 begin
	--выдаём пустой рекордсет с просьбой установить фильтры
	exec dbo.xp_frmStNewRS2_0 @S,@f,@Otv,@Rgm,@WhatFirm ,@SkladID,@FZ275,@WorkProblEl	
	RETURN
end 
else begin
	--подготовили таблицу
	if @WhatFirm = -100 begin
		exec xp_frmStNewRS2_0 @S,@f,@Otv,@Rgm,-100,@SkladID,@FZ275,@WorkProblEl	
	end
	else begin
		DECLARE @UPG bit
		DECLARE @LPG bit
		SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
				@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
		SET @FZ275 = 0
		SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус, Изготовитель,dbo.tblFirma.Firma as ГдеКупил,
		Заказано,dbo.SubSkladMin.MinKol as МинКоличество,ЦенаОптовая,ПримечаниеДолг,qSklad1C.SmKol as Количество,0 AS Свободно,
		ISNULL(dbo.qryElNotInSklad.Недополучено,0) AS Недополучено,FirstName,0 as IsDelayed,
		0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,dbo.qryAnalogActive.ID AS IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,VP5,NoAnalog,ISNULL(dbo.Sklad.FullNmeIzg, '') AS CreatorCode,CASE WHEN qMoveEl.DstID > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ExistMove,
		dbo.Sklad.UPKoef,dbo.Sklad.MinToBuy,dbo.Sklad.DfltIsp ,0 as TakeMe4St,
		case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
		FROM 
		(SELECT SkladID,SUM(Kol) as SmKol FROM dbo.Sklad1C with (nolock)
		WHERE dbo.Sklad1C.Who =CURRENT_USER 
		GROUP BY SkladID) as qSklad1C
		inner join dbo.Sklad with (nolock) ON qSklad1C.SkladID = dbo.Sklad.Код 
		inner join dbo.SubSkladMin with (nolock) ON dbo.Sklad.Код = dbo.SubSkladMin.SkladID and dbo.SubSkladMin.Isp = 8
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblFirma WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.Sklad.ГдеКупил = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryElNotInSklad ON dbo.Sklad.Код = dbo.qryElNotInSklad.SkladID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblFirmaAppendix WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblIspolnit01 WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv = dbo.tblIspolnit01.IspolnitID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryAnalogActive ON dbo.Sklad.Код = dbo.qryAnalogActive.SkladID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN
		(SELECT DstID FROM dbo.tblMoveEl WITH (NOLOCK)
         WHERE (Repl < 3) GROUP BY DstID) AS qMoveEl ON dbo.Sklad.Код = qMoveEl.DstID
        LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qrySharpName ON dbo.Sklad.Код = dbo.qrySharpName.SkladID AND CASE WHEN dbo.Sklad.ГдеКупил = '7825492099' THEN '9909040515' ELSE dbo.Sklad.ГдеКупил END = dbo.qrySharpName.UchetID
		left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.Sklad.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 
        where dbo.SubSkladMin.MinKol > qSklad1C.SmKol
        and  case when dbo.Sklad.ParentID>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
	end
	RETURN
end
END
USE [TrxSQL]
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRSa_0]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int =1,@WhatFirm smallint =0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm и далее оставлены для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
if @SkladID = -500 begin
	--выдаём пустой рекордсет с просьбой установить фильтры
	exec dbo.xp_frmStNewRS2_0 @S,@f,@Otv,@Rgm,@WhatFirm ,@SkladID,@FZ275,@WorkProblEl	
	RETURN
end 
else begin
	--подготовили таблицу
	if @WhatFirm = -100 begin
		exec xp_frmStNewRS2_0 @S,@f,@Otv,@Rgm,-100,@SkladID,@FZ275,@WorkProblEl	
	end
	else begin
		DECLARE @UPG bit
		DECLARE @LPG bit
		SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
				@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
		SET @FZ275 = 0
		SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус, Изготовитель,dbo.tblFirma.Firma as ГдеКупил,
		Заказано,dbo.SubSkladMin.MinKol as МинКоличество,ЦенаОптовая,ПримечаниеДолг,qSklad1C.SmKol as Количество,0 AS Свободно,
		ISNULL(dbo.qryElNotInSklad.Недополучено,0) AS Недополучено,FirstName,0 as IsDelayed,
		0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,dbo.qryAnalogActive.ID AS IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,VP5,NoAnalog,ISNULL(dbo.tblSklad.FullNmeIzg, '') AS CreatorCode,CASE WHEN qMoveEl.DstID > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ExistMove,
		dbo.tblSklad.UpKoef,dbo.tblSklad.MinToBuy,dbo.tblSklad.DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St,
		case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
		FROM 
		(SELECT SkladID,SUM(Kol) as SmKol FROM dbo.Sklad1C with (nolock)
		WHERE dbo.Sklad1C.Who =CURRENT_USER 
		GROUP BY SkladID) as qSklad1C
		inner join dbo.tblSklad with (nolock) ON qSklad1C.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код 
		inner join dbo.SubSkladMin with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.SubSkladMin.SkladID and dbo.SubSkladMin.Isp = 1
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblFirma WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryElNotInSklad ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qryElNotInSklad.SkladID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblFirmaAppendix WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblIspolnit01 WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv = dbo.tblIspolnit01.IspolnitID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryAnalogActive ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qryAnalogActive.SkladID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN
		(SELECT DstID FROM dbo.tblMoveEl WITH (NOLOCK)
         WHERE (Repl < 3) GROUP BY DstID) AS qMoveEl ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qMoveEl.DstID
        LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qrySharpName ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qrySharpName.SkladID AND CASE WHEN dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = '7825492099' THEN '9909040515' ELSE dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил END = dbo.qrySharpName.UchetID
		left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

        where dbo.SubSkladMin.MinKol > qSklad1C.SmKol
        and  case when dbo.tblSklad.ParentID>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
	end
	RETURN
end
END

USE [TrxSQL]
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRSb]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int =1,@WhatFirm smallint =0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm и далее оставлены для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
	CREATE TABLE #Lst (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Comm nvarchar(500),AppearMin datetime)
	insert into #Lst (SkladID,Comm,AppearMin )
	SELECT SkladID,Comm,AppearMin FROM dbo.SkladTracking with (nolock)
	WHERE Otv = @Otv and AppearMin > 0
	
	SELECT dbo.tblSklad.Код, dbo.tblSklad.Элемент, dbo.tblSklad.Серия, dbo.tblSklad.Наименование, dbo.tblSklad.Номинал, dbo.tblSklad.Корпус, dbo.tblSklad.Изготовитель,
	--по письму Филиппова от 27.08.24 (16.09.24)
	case when qTrash.KolTrash > dbo.tblSklad.Количество then 0 else dbo.tblSklad.Количество - isnull(qTrash.KolTrash,0) end as [Количество],  ISNULL(dbo.tblSklad.ПримечаниеДолг, '') AS ПримечаниеДолг, dbo.tblSklad.МинКоличество as [FZ275Kol],
	dbo.tblFirma.Firma AS ГдеКупил,ISNULL(dbo.qryElNotInSklad.Недополучено,0) AS Недополучено,
	--CASE WHEN ISNULL(qRes.Reserv, 0) + isnull(qryProblem.SumKol, 0) - dbo.tblSklad.Количество < 0 THEN 0 ELSE ISNULL(qRes.Reserv, 0) + isnull(qryProblem.SumKol, 0) - dbo.tblSklad.Количество  END AS Долг,
	case when qWarranty.CalcWarrantyDate>0 then convert(char(8),qWarranty.CalcWarrantyDate,4) else '' end as [Долг],
	dbo.tblSklad.VP5, dbo.tblSklad.NoAnalog,
	--ISNULL(dbo.tblStatWorkWithProblem.Sost, 0) AS SostProblEl,
	ISNULL(dbo.tblTehStock.kol, 0) AS TS,
	dbo.tblSklad.Серия as Analog,
	qPost.MinSrokPost as [OrderKat],case when qStart.MinStartPlan > 0 then convert(char(10),qStart.MinStartPlan,104) else '' end as [MinStartZak],
	qSel.Comm as CommProisv,qSel.Comm as SostProblEl,
	case when qSel.AppearMin > 0 then convert(char(8),qSel.AppearMin,4) else '' end as AppearMinTxt,
	dbo.tblSklad.Рекламации as Recl,dbo.tblSklad.Рекламации as DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St ,qStart.NeedOzn,
	case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
	FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qSel.SkladID 
	left outer join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryElNotInSklad ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qryElNotInSklad.SkladID 
	left outer join dbo.qryReservNew AS qRes WITH (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT     SUM(ISNULL(qrySch.SumKol, 0)) - SUM(ISNULL(qryAll.GetKol, 0)) AS SumKol, qrySch.SkladID
                            FROM          (SELECT     SUM(Количество) AS SumKol, SkladID, ShcID, AllID
                                                    FROM          dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)
                                                    WHERE      (IsProblem = 1)
                                                    GROUP BY SkladID, ShcID, AllID) AS qrySch LEFT OUTER JOIN
                                                       (SELECT     SUM(ISNULL(dbo.tblAll.Количество, 0)) AS GetKol, dbo.tblAll.SkladID, dbo.tblShceta.PrSchID, dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
                                                         FROM          dbo.tblAll WITH (NOLOCK) INNER JOIN
                                                                                dbo.tblShceta WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblShceta.ShcID = dbo.tblAll.ShcID
                                                         GROUP BY dbo.tblAll.SkladID, dbo.tblShceta.PrSchID, dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl) AS qryAll ON qryAll.PrSchID = qrySch.ShcID AND qryAll.SkladID = qrySch.SkladID AND 
                                                   qryAll.IDSchEl = qrySch.AllID
                            GROUP BY qrySch.SkladID) AS qryProblem ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qryProblem.SkladID
    --LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblStatWorkWithProblem WITH (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tblStatWorkWithProblem.SkladID 
    LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblTehStock WITH (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryAnalogActive ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qryAnalogActive.SkladID   
	left outer join
	(SELECT MIN(СрокПоставки) as MinSrokPost,SkladID FROM
	(SELECT dbo.tblSchEl.СрокПоставки,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID FROM dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	LEFT OUTER JOIN 
	(SELECT SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество) as Kol,IDSchEl FROM dbo.tblAll WITH (NOLOCK)
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblAll.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	GROUP BY dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl) as qAll ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = qAll.IDSchEl
	WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(qAll.Kol, 0) > 0) as q1
	GROUP BY SkladID) as qPost ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qPost.SkladID
	left outer join
	(select dbo.subtblRecomMat.SkladID,min(dbo.tblGraficRabot.BegPlan) as MinStartPlan,
	max(case when dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking.IDProcess>0 then 0 else 1 end) as NeedOzn
	 from dbo.subtblRecomMat with (nolock)
	inner join dbo.tblPosProcess with (nolock) ON dbo.subtblRecomMat.IDz = dbo.tblPosProcess.NamePlaty
	inner join dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock) ON dbo.tblPosProcess.ID = dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID
	inner join dbo.tblArb with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.SakasID = dbo.tblArb.IDSakas1
	inner join dbo.tblGraficRabot with (nolock) ON dbo.tblArb.IDArb = dbo.tblGraficRabot.WhatWork
	left outer join dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking with (nolock) ON dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking.IDProcess = dbo.tblPosProcess.IDProcess
	WHERE dbo.tblSakas1.SostSak < 4 and dbo.tblGraficRabot.BegPlan > 0
	GROUP BY dbo.subtblRecomMat.SkladID) as qStart ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qStart.SkladID
	left outer join
	(select min(case when FWarrantyDate>0 then FWarrantyDate else WarrantyDate end) as CalcWarrantyDate ,dbo.tblAll.SkladID
	FROM dbo.tblAllWarranty with (nolock) 	
	inner join dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblAllWarranty.IDAll = dbo.tblAll.AllID
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblAll.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	left outer join 
	(SELECT sum(Kolvo) as Kol,dbo.tblNakladSub.SkladID,dbo.tblNaborNakl.NKat 
	FROM dbo.tblNaborNakl with (nolock) 
	inner join dbo.tblNakladSub with (nolock) ON dbo.tblNaborNakl.IDElInNakl = dbo.tblNakladSub.IDNaklSub 
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblNakladSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	WHERE dbo.tblNaborNakl.TipUpak = 255
	GROUP BY dbo.tblNakladSub.SkladID,dbo.tblNaborNakl.NKat) as qUsed ON dbo.tblAllWarranty.ID = qUsed.NKat and dbo.tblAll.SkladID = qUsed.SkladID 
	WHERE NumPart = '--IDAll--' and (FWarrantyDate>0 OR WarrantyDate>0)
	and dbo.tblAllWarranty.Kol > isnull(qUsed.Kol,0)
	group by dbo.tblAll.SkladID) as qWarranty ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qWarranty.SkladID
	left outer join
	(select sum([Резерв]) as KolTrash,SkladID FROM dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
	WHERE ResID = 8579 and [Прим] = 'просроченный элемент'
	GROUP BY SkladID) as qTrash ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qTrash.SkladID 
	left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

	where dbo.tblSklad.Количество + ISNULL(dbo.qryElNotInSklad.Недополучено,0) < dbo.tblSklad.МинКоличество
    ORDER BY dbo.tblSklad.Элемент, dbo.tblSklad.Серия, dbo.tblSklad.Наименование
    DROP TABLE #Lst
	RETURN

END

USE [TrxSQL]
GO

-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRSb_0]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int =1,@WhatFirm smallint =0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm и далее оставлены для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
	CREATE TABLE #Lst (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Comm nvarchar(500),AppearMin datetime,AppearMinByAdd bit)
	insert into #Lst (SkladID,Comm,AppearMin,AppearMinByAdd )
	SELECT SkladID,Comm,AppearMin,AppearMinByAdd FROM dbo.SkladTracking with (nolock)
	WHERE Otv = @Otv
	
	SELECT dbo.tblSklad.Код, dbo.tblSklad.Элемент, dbo.tblSklad.Серия, dbo.tblSklad.Наименование, dbo.tblSklad.Номинал, dbo.tblSklad.Корпус, dbo.tblSklad.Изготовитель,
	--по письму Филиппова от 27.08.24 (16.09.24)
	case when qTrash.KolTrash > dbo.tblSklad.Количество then 0 else dbo.tblSklad.Количество - isnull(qTrash.KolTrash,0) end as [Количество], ISNULL(dbo.tblSklad.ПримечаниеДолг, '') AS ПримечаниеДолг, dbo.tblSklad.МинКоличество as [FZ275Kol],
	dbo.tblFirma.Firma AS ГдеКупил,ISNULL(dbo.qryElNotInSklad.Недополучено,0) AS Недополучено,
	--CASE WHEN ISNULL(qRes.Reserv, 0) + isnull(qryProblem.SumKol, 0) - dbo.tblSklad.Количество < 0 THEN 0 ELSE ISNULL(qRes.Reserv, 0) + isnull(qryProblem.SumKol, 0) - dbo.tblSklad.Количество  END AS Долг,
	case when qWarranty.CalcWarrantyDate>0 then convert(char(8),qWarranty.CalcWarrantyDate,4) else '' end as [Долг],
	dbo.tblSklad.VP5, dbo.tblSklad.NoAnalog,
	--ISNULL(dbo.tblStatWorkWithProblem.Sost, 0) AS SostProblEl,
	ISNULL(dbo.tblTehStock.kol, 0) AS TS,
	dbo.tblSklad.Серия as Analog,
	qPost.MinSrokPost as [OrderKat],case when qStart.MinStartPlan > 0 then convert(char(10),qStart.MinStartPlan,104) else '' end as [MinStartZak],
	qSel.Comm as CommProisv,qSel.Comm as SostProblEl,
	case when qSel.AppearMin > 0 then convert(char(8),qSel.AppearMin,4) else '' end as AppearMinTxt,
	case when qSel.AppearMin > 0 then qSel.AppearMinByAdd else 0 end as AppearMinByAdd,
	dbo.tblSklad.Рекламации as Recl,dbo.tblSklad.Рекламации as DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St ,qStart.NeedOzn,
	case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
	FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qSel.SkladID 
	left outer join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryElNotInSklad ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qryElNotInSklad.SkladID 
	left outer join dbo.qryReservNew AS qRes WITH (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT     SUM(ISNULL(qrySch.SumKol, 0)) - SUM(ISNULL(qryAll.GetKol, 0)) AS SumKol, qrySch.SkladID
                            FROM          (SELECT     SUM(Количество) AS SumKol, SkladID, ShcID, AllID
                                                    FROM          dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)
                                                    WHERE      (IsProblem = 1)
                                                    GROUP BY SkladID, ShcID, AllID) AS qrySch LEFT OUTER JOIN
                                                       (SELECT     SUM(ISNULL(dbo.tblAll.Количество, 0)) AS GetKol, dbo.tblAll.SkladID, dbo.tblShceta.PrSchID, dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
                                                         FROM          dbo.tblAll WITH (NOLOCK) INNER JOIN
                                                                                dbo.tblShceta WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblShceta.ShcID = dbo.tblAll.ShcID
                                                         GROUP BY dbo.tblAll.SkladID, dbo.tblShceta.PrSchID, dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl) AS qryAll ON qryAll.PrSchID = qrySch.ShcID AND qryAll.SkladID = qrySch.SkladID AND 
                                                   qryAll.IDSchEl = qrySch.AllID
                            GROUP BY qrySch.SkladID) AS qryProblem ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qryProblem.SkladID
    --LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblStatWorkWithProblem WITH (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tblStatWorkWithProblem.SkladID 
    LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblTehStock WITH (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.qryAnalogActive ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.qryAnalogActive.SkladID   
	left outer join
	(SELECT MIN(СрокПоставки) as MinSrokPost,SkladID FROM
	(SELECT dbo.tblSchEl.СрокПоставки,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID FROM dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	LEFT OUTER JOIN 
	(SELECT SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество) as Kol,IDSchEl FROM dbo.tblAll WITH (NOLOCK)
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblAll.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	GROUP BY dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl) as qAll ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = qAll.IDSchEl
	WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(qAll.Kol, 0) > 0) as q1
	GROUP BY SkladID) as qPost ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qPost.SkladID
	left outer join
	(select dbo.subtblRecomMat.SkladID,min(dbo.tblGraficRabot.BegPlan) as MinStartPlan,
	max(case when dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking.IDProcess>0 then 0 else 1 end) as NeedOzn
	 from dbo.subtblRecomMat with (nolock)
	inner join dbo.tblPosProcess with (nolock) ON dbo.subtblRecomMat.IDz = dbo.tblPosProcess.NamePlaty
	inner join dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock) ON dbo.tblPosProcess.ID = dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID
	inner join dbo.tblArb with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.SakasID = dbo.tblArb.IDSakas1
	inner join dbo.tblGraficRabot with (nolock) ON dbo.tblArb.IDArb = dbo.tblGraficRabot.WhatWork
	left outer join dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking with (nolock) ON dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking.IDProcess = dbo.tblPosProcess.IDProcess
	WHERE dbo.tblSakas1.SostSak < 4 and dbo.tblGraficRabot.BegPlan > 0
	GROUP BY dbo.subtblRecomMat.SkladID) as qStart ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qStart.SkladID
	left outer join
	(select min(case when FWarrantyDate>0 then FWarrantyDate else WarrantyDate end) as CalcWarrantyDate ,dbo.tblAll.SkladID
	FROM dbo.tblAllWarranty with (nolock) 	
	inner join dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblAllWarranty.IDAll = dbo.tblAll.AllID
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblAll.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	left outer join 
	(SELECT sum(Kolvo) as Kol,dbo.tblNakladSub.SkladID,dbo.tblNaborNakl.NKat 
	FROM dbo.tblNaborNakl with (nolock) 
	inner join dbo.tblNakladSub with (nolock) ON dbo.tblNaborNakl.IDElInNakl = dbo.tblNakladSub.IDNaklSub 
	inner join #Lst as qSel ON dbo.tblNakladSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
	WHERE dbo.tblNaborNakl.TipUpak = 255
	GROUP BY dbo.tblNakladSub.SkladID,dbo.tblNaborNakl.NKat) as qUsed ON dbo.tblAllWarranty.ID = qUsed.NKat and dbo.tblAll.SkladID = qUsed.SkladID 
	WHERE NumPart = '--IDAll--' and (FWarrantyDate>0 OR WarrantyDate>0)
	and dbo.tblAllWarranty.Kol > isnull(qUsed.Kol,0)
	group by dbo.tblAll.SkladID) as qWarranty ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qWarranty.SkladID
	left outer join
	(select sum([Резерв]) as KolTrash,SkladID FROM dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
	WHERE ResID = 8579 and [Прим] = 'просроченный элемент'
	GROUP BY SkladID) as qTrash ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qTrash.SkladID 
	left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

    ORDER BY dbo.tblSklad.Элемент, dbo.tblSklad.Серия, dbo.tblSklad.Наименование
    DROP TABLE #Lst
	RETURN

END

USE [TrxSQL]
GO
