ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS1_1]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int =0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@BegDta datetime='20000101',@EndDta datetime='20000101',@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET DATEFORMAT DMY;
DECLARE @Dta1 datetime
DECLARE @Dta2 datetime
SET @Dta1 = CAST(convert(char(8),@BegDta,112) + ' 00:00:00' as datetime)
SET @Dta2 = CAST(convert(char(8),@EndDta,112) + ' 23:59:59' as datetime)
DECLARE @BDta datetime
DECLARE @EDta datetime
DECLARE @KolDolg int
DECLARE @Dta datetime
DECLARE @tmp int
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
DECLARE @IsZakup bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end
SET @IsZakup = 0 
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirmaShort 
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirmaShort 
		left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
	--ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
	if @f = '9909041763' begin
		SELECT @IsZakup = case when COUNT(*)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 
		FROM dbo.SubPermissions with (nolock)
		WHERE IDPerm = 'MoveEl4Stat' and usr = CURRENT_USER 
	end
end

CREATE TABLE #TmpAvt (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Avt tinyint)
SET @Dta =GETDATE()
--and @IsZakup = 0 для ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	SET @KolDolg = -2147483648
	INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
	SELECT DstID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblMoveEl with (nolock)
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
	FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
	WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0
	GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qRes.SkladID 
	inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
	inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
	WHERE dbo.tblMoveEl.Repl < 3
end
else begin
	SET @KolDolg = 0
	if @SkladID =0 begin
		if @FZ275 = 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579 and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >= 0
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ = 1
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.ParentID >=0 
		end
		if @FZ275 = -1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID > 0 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275 > 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID =@FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
	end 
	else begin
		if @SkladID =-500 begin
			SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
			Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,0 as Долг,
			0,'' as FirstName,0 as ProblKol,
			'' as UchetID,0 as FZ275Kol,
			'' as SharpName,0 as NotRec,'' as NmeCat,
			'' as [CreatorCode],'' as Примечание,0 as [Активный],
			0 as FullDolg,'0' as Analog ,0 as IDInvoice ,
			VP5,NoAnalog,'' as WhatFirmTxt,0 as ExistMove,
			0 as SostProblEl,'' as SrcProblEl
			FROM  dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK)  WHERE Код = -500
			RETURN
			--=IIf([Недополучено]-[Долг]-[TS]<0;[Долг]-[Недополучено]+[TS];0)
		end
		else begin
		if @FZ275=0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID 
		end
		if @FZ275=-1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID > 0
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275>0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID = @FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		end
	end
end
SET @EDta = GETDATE()
SET @BDta = DATEADD(YEAR,-1,@EDta)
if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,
--,0 as IsBoth,
CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,
SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0) as OrderKat,
isnull(qryTS.Kol,0) as TS,
--isnull(qryTS.KolPok,0) as KolPok,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog,qrySklad.Avt , FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],Примечание,[Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,WhatFirmTxt ,
CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as WF,
qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,
cast(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
			qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено
			END
		END
	END
END as int) as txtSV,qryFrmSt.UpKoef,qryFrmSt.MinToBuy,qryFrmSt.MainPlace
--,CURRENT_USER 
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak , Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,
CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '0         0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [KolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,NU,flgNU,f1 as KolInRes,UPKoef,MinToBuy,MainPlace


FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own  
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
--AND (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.FirstName,'') Like @Otv)
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
INNER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

--left outer join
--(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
--inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
--WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
--GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 

WHERE
(qryfrmSt.FullDolg-qryfrmSt.Недополучено + ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) >@KolDolg )
AND
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
AND dbo.IsGuiltyResByIntervalSQL(Код,@Dta1,@Dta2)=1
ORDER BY 
--isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , 
qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
RETURN
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,NotRec,NmeCat,0 as OrderKat,0 as TS,0 as KolPok,VP5
,NoAnalog,qrySklad.Avt,FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],
Примечание, [Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,
cast(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
			qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено
			END
		END
	END
END as int) as txtSV,qryFrmSt.UpKoef,qryFrmSt.MainPlace
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak , Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '0         0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [KolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SrcProblEl,flgNU,KolInRes, 0 as TS,UpKoef,MinToBuy,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.MainPlace
FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
INNER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
left outer join
(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 
WHERE
(qryfrmSt.[Долг]-qryfrmSt.[Недополучено]>0) 
AND
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
AND dbo.IsGuiltyResByIntervalSQL(Код,@Dta1,@Dta2)=1
ORDER BY isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=3 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,NotRec,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0)  as OrderKat,NmeCat,ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--isnull(qryTS.KolPok,0) as KolPok 
0 as KolPok
,VP5
,NoAnalog,qrySklad.Avt,FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],Примечание, [Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,
cast(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
	CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
			qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryTS.kol,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено
			END
		END
	END
END as int) as txtSV,qryFrmSt.UpKoef,qryFrmSt.MainPlace
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak , Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '0         0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [KolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SrcProblEl,NU,flgNU,KolRes4OverKol as KolInRes,UpKoef,MinToBuy ,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.MainPlace
FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt 
INNER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 
left outer join
(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 
WHERE
(qryfrmSt.FullDolg-qryfrmSt.Недополучено + CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Элемент='Давальческое сырье' THEN 0 ELSE ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) END >@KolDolg )
AND
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
AND dbo.IsGuiltyResByIntervalSQL(Код,@Dta1,@Dta2)=1
ORDER BY isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
drop table  #TmpFrm
drop table #TmpAvt 
END

GO
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS1]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int =0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @BDta datetime
DECLARE @EDta datetime
DECLARE @KolDolg int
DECLARE @Dta datetime
DECLARE @tmp int
DECLARE @IsZakup bit
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end
SET @IsZakup = 0 
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0

CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirmaShort 
		left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
	--ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
	if @f = '9909041763' begin
		SELECT @IsZakup = case when COUNT(*)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 
		FROM dbo.SubPermissions with (nolock)
		WHERE IDPerm = 'MoveEl4Stat' and usr = CURRENT_USER 
	end
end

SET @Dta =GETDATE()
	
CREATE TABLE #TmpEls (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY)
if @Rgm = 1 begin
	insert into #TmpEls (SkladID )
	select qryfrmSt.Код 
	FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own  as qryfrmSt
	--inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON qryfrmSt.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
	WHERE 
	(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
	OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
	and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
	and
	(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.f1 =0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.f1 - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
				CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
				END
			END
		END
	END  >@KolDolg)
	and qryfrmSt.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
end
if @Rgm = 2 begin
	insert into #TmpEls (SkladID)
	select qryfrmSt.Код 
	FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav  as qryfrmSt
	WHERE
	(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
	OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
	and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
	and
	(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolInRes =0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolInRes - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
				CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE 0 END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
				END
			END
		END
	END>0) 
	and qryfrmSt.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
end
if @Rgm = 3 begin
	insert into #TmpEls (SkladID)
	select qryfrmSt.Код 
	FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0  as qryfrmSt
	WHERE 
	(isnull(qryfrmSt.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(qryfrmSt.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
	OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(qryfrmSt.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
	and (qryfrmSt.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
	and
	(CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolRes4OverKol =0 THEN 0 ELSE
		CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.KolWOUpak + qryfrmSt.Недополучено - qryfrmSt.KolRes4OverKol - CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.ProblKol >=0 THEN 0 ELSE
			CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END - qryfrmSt.Недополучено <=0 THEN 0 ELSE
				CASE WHEN flgNU = 1 and @FZ275 = 0 THEN NU*CEILING(((qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено)/NU)) ELSE 
				--введено по ТЗ 577 Маслюкова (12.04.23)
				case when qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено < qryfrmSt.MinToBuy then qryfrmSt.MinToBuy
				else qryfrmSt.Долг + CASE WHEN @FZ275>0 THEN 0 ELSE isnull(qryfrmSt.TS,0) END-qryfrmSt.Недополучено end
				END
			END
		END
	END > @KolDolg )
	and qryfrmSt.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
end	
	
CREATE TABLE #TmpAvt (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Avt tinyint)
--and @IsZakup = 0 для ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	SET @KolDolg = -2147483648
	INSERT INTO #TmpAvt  (SkladID,Avt)
	SELECT DstID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblMoveEl with (nolock)
		inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qSel.SkladID 
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
	FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
	WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0
	GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qRes.SkladID 
	inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
	inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
	WHERE dbo.tblMoveEl.Repl < 3
end
else begin
	SET @KolDolg = 0
	if @SkladID =0 begin
		if @FZ275 = 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt  (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
				inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qSel.SkladID 
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579 and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >= 0
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ = 1
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.ParentID >=0 
		end
		if @FZ275 = -1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) 
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID > 0 
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes 
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275 > 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join #TmpEls as qSel ON dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = qSel.SkladID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID =@FZ275 
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes 
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
	end 
	else begin
		if @SkladID =-500 begin
			SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
			Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,0 as Долг,
			0,'' as FirstName,0 as ProblKol,
			'' as UchetID,0 as FZ275Kol,
			'' as SharpName,0 as NotRec,'' as NmeCat,
			'' as [CreatorCode],'' as Примечание,0 as [Активный],
			0 as FullDolg,'0' as Analog ,0 as IDInvoice ,
			VP5,NoAnalog,'' as WhatFirmTxt,0 as ExistMove,
			0 as SostProblEl,'' as SrcProblEl
			FROM  dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK)  WHERE Код = -500
			RETURN
			--=IIf([Недополучено]-[Долг]-[TS]<0;[Долг]-[Недополучено]+[TS];0)
		end
		else begin
		if @FZ275=0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID 
		end
		if @FZ275=-1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) 
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID > 0
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
		end
		if @FZ275>0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT SkladID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) 
			FROM
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.FZ275ID = @FZ275 
			GROUP BY dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID ) as qRes 
			inner join dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) ON qRes.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			 --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		end
	end
end
SET @EDta = GETDATE()
SET @BDta = DATEADD(YEAR,-1,@EDta)
if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUPak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
0 as IsDelayed,
UchetID,
--,0 as IsBoth,
CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,
SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0) as OrderKat,
isnull(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--isnull(qryTS.KolPok,0) as KolPok,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog,qryfrmSt.Avt , FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],Примечание,[Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,WhatFirmTxt ,
CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as WF,
qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,MainPlace,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
--,CURRENT_USER 
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUPak,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,
CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--включил обратно по письму Семёнова от 29.06.23
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
--'-1        0         ' as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,qrySklad.Avt,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.RequestSch,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.MainPlace
FROM #tmpAvt as qrySklad
inner join dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own ON qrySklad.SkladID = dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Код 
--inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.UchetID = qFrm.UchID 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
--AND (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.UchetID,'') Like @f)
--AND (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.FirstName,'') Like @Otv)
--AND (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.OtvSklad >= @LBOtv and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.OtvSklad<=@UBOtv)
--AND (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.OtvSklad = @Otv or @Otv=0)
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Own.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
) as qryfrmSt

left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,qLst.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
inner join #TmpAvt as qLst ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qLst.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, qLst.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON qryfrmSt.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 
WHERE
(qryfrmSt.FullDolg + ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) >@KolDolg )
AND
(isnull(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint),0) = @WhatFirm OR isnull(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint),0) < 0)

ORDER BY 
--isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , 
qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
RETURN
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг, Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
0 as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,NotRec,NmeCat,0 as OrderKat,0 as TS,0 as KolPok,VP5
,NoAnalog,qryfrmSt.Avt,FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],
Примечание, [Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,MainPlace,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUPak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--включил обратно по письму Семёнова от 29.06.23
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWoUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
--'-1        0         ' as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SrcProblEl,UPKoef,MinToBuy ,0 as DfltIsp,qrySklad.Avt,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.RequestSch,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.MainPlace
FROM #TmpAvt as qrySklad 
inner join  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav ON qrySklad.SkladID = dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
--AND (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.OtvSklad = @Otv or @Otv=0)
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
) as qryfrmSt
left outer join
(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,qLst.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
inner join #TmpAvt as qLst ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qLst.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, qLst.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON qryfrmSt.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(qryfrmSt.[Долг]>0) 
AND
(isnull(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint),0) = @WhatFirm OR isnull(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint),0) < 0)
ORDER BY isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=3 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг, Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
0 as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,NotRec,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0)  as OrderKat,NmeCat,ISNULL(qryTS.Kol,0) as TS,
--isnull(qryTS.KolPok,0) as KolPok 
0 as KolPok,VP5
,NoAnalog,qryfrmSt.Avt,FZ275Kol,
[CreatorCode],Примечание, [Активный],Analog ,IDInvoice ,qryfrmSt.ExistMove,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,MinToBuy ,DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END as Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice,
VP5,NoAnalog,
--отключено по причине медленного выполнения (06.10.22)
--включил обратно по письму Семёнова от 29.06.23
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [Количество]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
--'-1        0         ' as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SrcProblEl,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.UpKoef,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.MinToBuy ,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.DfltIsp,qrySklad.Avt,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.RequestSch,dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.MainPlace
FROM #tmpAvt as qrySklad
inner join dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0 ON qrySklad.SkladID = dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Код
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
--AND (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.OtvSklad = @Otv or @Otv=0)
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
) as qryfrmSt 
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

left outer join
(select MIN(dbo.tblReserv.Дата ) as MinDatePost, SkladID from dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
WHERE dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.ReadyDate ='19000101' and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 and dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579
GROUP BY SkladID  ) as qSort ON qryfrmSt.Код = qSort.SkladID 

left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,qLst.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
inner join #TmpAvt as qLst ON dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID = qLst.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, qLst.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON qryfrmSt.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(qryfrmSt.FullDolg+ CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Элемент='Давальческое сырье' THEN 0 ELSE ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) END >@KolDolg )
AND
(isnull(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint),0) = @WhatFirm OR isnull(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint),0) < 0)
ORDER BY isnull(qSort.MinDatePost,'20991231') , qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
drop table #TmpFrm 
drop table #TmpAvt
END

/*источник данных подчиненной формы frmMinimum формы FrmSt 


*/
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS2]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint = 0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm и далее оставлены для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS 
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
declare @tmp int
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
end

DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 0
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус, Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,МинКоличество,ЦенаОптовая,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,Свободно,
Недополучено,FirstName,0 as IsDelayed,0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,VP5
,NoAnalog,CreatorCode,ExistMove,UpKoef,MinToBuy ,DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,
case when [МинКоличество] > KolWOTrash + [Недополучено] then [МинКоличество] - KolWOTrash - [Недополучено] else 0 end as OrderEls,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS2
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE ((isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Серия,'') LIKE @S) OR (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Наименование,'') LIKE @S) OR
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Номинал,'') LIKE @S) OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код as varchar(10)))>0)
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Элемент<>'Давальческое сырье'
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS2.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус, Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,МинКоличество,ЦенаОптовая,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,Свободно,
Недополучено,FirstName,0 as IsDelayed,0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,VP5
,NoAnalog,CreatorCode,ExistMove,UpKoef ,MinToBuy,0 as DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,
case when [МинКоличество] > KolWOTrash + [Недополучено] then [МинКоличество] - KolWOTrash - [Недополучено] else 0 end as OrderEls,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS2
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE ((isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Серия,'') LIKE @S) OR (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Наименование,'') LIKE @S) OR
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Номинал,'') LIKE @S) OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код as varchar(10)))>0)
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Элемент='Давальческое сырье'
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS2.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=3 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус, Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,МинКоличество,ЦенаОптовая,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,Свободно,
Недополучено,FirstName,0 as IsDelayed,0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,VP5
,NoAnalog,CreatorCode,ExistMove,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,
case when [МинКоличество] > KolWOTrash + [Недополучено] then [МинКоличество] - KolWOTrash - [Недополучено] else 0 end as OrderEls,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS2
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE ((isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Серия,'') LIKE @S) OR (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Наименование,'') LIKE @S) OR
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Номинал,'') LIKE @S) OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Код as varchar(10)))>0)
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS2.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS2.Наименование
end
drop table #TmpFrm 

END

/*источник данных подчиненной формы frmMinimum формы FrmSt */
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS3]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint =0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm оставлена для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS 
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @tmp int
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma  with (nolock)
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma  with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
end
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 0
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,Заказано,
МинКоличество,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,
FirstName,ProblKol,KolZak,0 as IsDelayed,
0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,VP5,NoAnalog,
CreatorCode,ExistMove,SostProblEl,UPKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,
case when [МинКоличество] > KolWOTrash + [Недополучено] then [МинКоличество] - KolWOTrash - [Недополучено] else 0 end as OrderEls,
TS,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk,mainplace
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS3 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS3.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON  dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Код as varchar(10)))>0)
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Элемент<>'Давальческое сырье'
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS3.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS3.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,Заказано,
МинКоличество,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,
FirstName,ProblKol,KolZak ,0 as IsDelayed,
0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,VP5,NoAnalog,CreatorCode,ExistMove,SostProblEl,UPKoef ,MinToBuy,0 as DfltIsp ,qTake.TakeMeSost as TakeMe4St,
RequestSch,
case when [МинКоличество] > KolWOTrash + [Недополучено] then [МинКоличество] - KolWOTrash - [Недополучено] else 0 end as OrderEls,
TS,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk,mainplace
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS3 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS3.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join
(SELECT MAX(Sost) TakeMeSost,SkladID FROM
(SELECT  CASE WHEN dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END as Sost,dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID
FROM    dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblAll with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.AllID = dbo.tblAll.IDSchEl
GROUP BY dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate, dbo.tblSchEl.SkladID, dbo.tblSchEl.Количество
HAVING  (dbo.tblSchEl.Количество - ISNULL(SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество), 0) > 0) AND (dbo.tblSchEl.TakeMeDate <GETDATE())) as q
group by SkladID) as qTake ON dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Код = qTake.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON  dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 
WHERE
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Код as varchar(10)))>0)
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Элемент='Давальческое сырье'
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS3.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS3.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS3.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=3 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,Заказано,
МинКоличество,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak  AS Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,

		inner join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
end
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 0
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ЦенаОптовая,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,Недополучено,FirstName,0 as IsDelayed,
0 as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,VP5,NoAnalog,CreatorCode ,ExistMove,UPKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St,

FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS5 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS5.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS5.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
((isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS5.Серия,'') LIKE @S)  OR (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS5.Наименование,'') LIKE @S) OR
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS5.Номинал,'') LIKE @S) OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS5.Код as varchar(10)))>0)
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS5.Элемент <> 'Давальческое сырье'
and dbo.qryfrmStRS5.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS6]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int=0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
--@WhatFirm и далее оставлены для совместимости с процедурой xp_frmStRS1_0
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @tmp int
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
end
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0  THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,
case when @PaintDolg = 0  THEN 0 ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL3([Код],@WhatFirm) END as IsBoth,
SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,VP5,NoAnalog,CreatorCode ,ExistMove,UPKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 
WHERE
 (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Элемент<>'Давальческое сырье'
AND (dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL2([Код],@WhatFirm) = @WhatFirm OR dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL2([Код],@WhatFirm)<0)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak  AS Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0  THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,
case when @PaintDolg = 0  THEN 0 ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL3([Код],@WhatFirm) END as IsBoth,
SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,VP5,NoAnalog,CreatorCode ,ExistMove,UPKoef,MinToBuy,0 as DfltIsp ,0 as TakeMe4St,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

 WHERE
 (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
AND dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Элемент='Давальческое сырье'
AND (dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL2([Код],@WhatFirm) = @WhatFirm OR dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL2([Код],@WhatFirm)<0)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=3 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,KolWOUpak AS Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0  THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,
case when @PaintDolg = 0  THEN 0 ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL3([Код],@WhatFirm) END as IsBoth,
SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,VP5,NoAnalog,CreatorCode ,ExistMove,UPKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp ,0 as TakeMe4St,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

 WHERE
 (isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
AND (dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL2([Код],@WhatFirm) = @WhatFirm OR dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL2([Код],@WhatFirm)<0)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
ORDER BY dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Элемент, dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Серия, dbo.qryfrmStRS6_0.Наименование
end

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
