ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_frmStNewRS8]
(@S nvarchar(255)='%',@f nvarchar(100)='%',@Otv int =0,@Rgm int = 1,@WhatFirm smallint=0,@SkladID int=0,@FZ275 int=0,@WorkProblEl tinyint=255)
AS
BEGIN 
SET NOCOUNT ON;
DECLARE @BDta datetime
DECLARE @EDta datetime
DECLARE @KolDolg int
DECLARE @Who varchar(20)
declare @tmp int
DECLARE @IsZakup bit
SET @IsZakup = 0 
--@FZ275 отключено. Вместо него пока работаю по галке "ПГ", согласно заданию Реннике от 05.12.23
DECLARE @UPG bit
DECLARE @LPG bit
SELECT	@UPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 1 WHEN -2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
		@LPG = CASE @FZ275 WHEN 0 THEN 0 WHEN -3 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END
SET @FZ275 = 0
CREATE TABLE #TmpFrm (UchID varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY)
if @f = '%' begin
	INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
	SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
	left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
	where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
end
else begin
	if @f='MultiSelect' begin
		SET @tmp=0
		SELECT @tmp = COUNT(*) FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147
		and vl = '9909041763'
		if @tmp >0 begin
			SET @f = '9909041763'
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			values (@f)
		end
		else begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
			SELECT cast(vl as varchar(12)) FROM
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 0
			UNION ALL
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM 
			dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
			inner join
			(SELECT vl FROM dbo.tmpIDs WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Who = CURRENT_USER AND Tip = 147 AND ID = 1) as qSel ON dbo.tblFirma.Holding = qSel.vl ) as q
			left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON q.Vl = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID
			where (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
		end
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO #TmpFrm (UchID)
		--values (@f)
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.UchetID FROM dbo.tblFirma with (nolock)
		left outer join dbo.tblFirmaAppendix with (nolock) ON dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.UchetID 
		where  dbo.tblFirma.UchetID = @f AND (ISNULL(dbo.tblFirmaAppendix.SkladOtv,0) = @Otv or @Otv=0)
	end
	--ТЗ 591 Реннике (05.06.23)
	if @f = '9909041763' begin
		SELECT @IsZakup = case when COUNT(*)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END 
		FROM dbo.SubPermissions with (nolock)
		WHERE IDPerm = 'MoveEl4Stat' and usr = CURRENT_USER 
	end
end
DECLARE @PaintDolg bit
if left(@S,1)='&' begin
	--включил насильно по письму Семёнова от 23.08.23
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
	SET @S = RIGHT(@S,len(@S)-1)
end
else begin
	SET @PaintDolg = 1
end

SET @Who = CURRENT_USER 
CREATE TABLE #TmpAvt (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY,Avt tinyint)
if @f = '9909041763' and @IsZakup = 0 begin
	SET @KolDolg = -2147483648
	INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
	SELECT DstID,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblMoveEl with (nolock)
	LEFT OUTER JOIN
	(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
	INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
	WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0
	GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblMoveEl.DstID = qRes.SkladID 
	
	WHERE Repl < 3
end
else begin
	SET @KolDolg = 0
	if @SkladID =0 begin
		if @FZ275 = 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.IDRes <> 8579 and dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >= 0
			AND dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ = 1
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.ParentID >=0 
		end
		if @FZ275 = -1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID  
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275 > 0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad with (nolock)
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID =@FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID
			inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.tblSklad.ГдеКупил = qFrm.UchID 
			--WHERE dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.VP5 = 1
		end
	end 
	else begin
		if @SkladID =-500 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt)
			VALUES (-500,0,@Who )
		end
		else begin
		if @FZ275=0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			LEFT OUTER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID 
		end
		if @FZ275=-1 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2) FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID > 0
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		if @FZ275>0 begin
			INSERT INTO #TmpAvt (SkladID,Avt )
			SELECT Код,isnull(qRes.Avt,2)  FROM dbo.tblSklad WITH (NOLOCK) 
			INNER JOIN
			(SELECT MAX(CAST(dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt as tinyint)) as Avt,dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID 
			FROM dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK)
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK)ON dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes 
			WHERE dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0 AND dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @SkladID 
			AND dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID = @FZ275 
			GROUP BY SkladID ) as qRes ON dbo.tblSklad.Код = qRes.SkladID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSklad.Код = @SkladID --AND dbo.tblSklad.VP5 = 1
		end
		end
	end
end
SET @EDta = GETDATE()
SET @BDta = DATEADD(YEAR,-1,@EDta)
if @Rgm=1 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,NmeCat,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0) as OrderKat,isnull(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--qryTS.KolPok ,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog ,qrySklad.Avt ,
[CreatorCode], qryfrmSt.ExistMove,qryfrmSt.FZ275Kol,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.MinToBuy,qryfrmSt.DfltIsp,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS8.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St 
FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS8
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS8.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and (dbo.qryfrmStRS8.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl=255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS8.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
	(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
	--отключён по письму Знака от 16.05.19
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND 
--([FullDolg] + ISNULL(qryTS.TS,0) >@KolDolg )
ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=2 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,0 as OrderKat,0 as TS,0 as KolPok,VP5,
NoAnalog,qrySklad.Avt ,
[CreatorCode],qryfrmSt.ExistMove,qryfrmSt.FZ275Kol,
qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.MinToBuy,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice ,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy ,0 as DfltIsp,0 as TakeMe4St 
FROM  dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav 
inner join #TmpFrm as qFrm ON dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.UchetID = qFrm.UchID
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 

and (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl = 255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0_Dav.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID 
inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 
WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND ([Долг]>@KolDolg )

ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
if @Rgm=3 begin
SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN 0 ELSE dbo.IsDelayedElByResSQL([Код]) END as IsDelayed,
UchetID,CAST(RIGHT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) as IsBoth,SharpName,NotRec,IDInvoice,Analog,
isnull(qryKat.KolInKat,0)  as OrderKat,ISNULL(qryTS.kol,0) as TS,
--qryTS.KolPok ,
0 as KolPok,
VP5,NoAnalog,qrySklad.Avt ,
[CreatorCode],qryfrmSt.ExistMove,qryfrmSt.SostProblEl,qryfrmSt.FZ275Kol,qryfrmSt.SrcProblEl,qryfrmSt.UpKoef,qryfrmSt.MinToBuy,
case when dbo.tmpIDs.ID>0 then 1 else 0 end as chk
FROM 
(SELECT Код,Элемент,Серия,Наименование,Номинал,Корпус,Изготовитель,ГдеКупил,
Заказано,ПримечаниеДолг,kolWOUpak as Количество,ЦенаОптовая,Долг,Недополучено,FirstName,ProblKol,
UchetID,ISNULL(KolFZ275,0) as FZ275Kol,
dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SharpName,NotRec,NmeCat,
isnull(FullNmeIzg,'') as [CreatorCode],Примечание,AnalogActive as [Активный],
FullDolg,Analog ,IDInvoice,
VP5,NoAnalog,
case when @PaintDolg = 0 THEN '-1        0         ' ELSE dbo.GetWhatFirmSQL6([Код],@WhatFirm,[Долг]-CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END,CASE WHEN Недополучено>0 THEN Недополучено ELSE 0 END + [kolWOUpak]) END as WhatFirmTxt,
ExistMove,
SostProblEl,SrcProblEl,UpKoef,MinToBuy,DfltIsp ,0 as TakeMe4St 
FROM dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0 
WHERE 
(isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Серия,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Наименование,'') LIKE @S OR isnull(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Номинал,'') LIKE @S
 OR PATINDEX(@S,CAST(dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.Код as varchar(10)))>0) 
and  (dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.SostProblEl= @WorkProblEl OR @WorkProblEl = 255)
and dbo.qryfrmStRS1_0.PG BETWEEN @LPG AND @UPG
) as qryfrmSt 
LEFT OUTER JOIN 
(SELECT SkladID,Avt FROM #TmpAvt WITH (NOLOCK)) as qrySklad ON qryfrmSt.Код = qrySklad.SkladID
--LEFT OUTER JOIN
--(SELECT dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID, 
--CASE WHEN dbo.tblTehStock.kol is null THEN CASE WHEN ISNULL(qryPok.KolPok,0)>2 THEN isnull(qryKat.KolInKat ,5000) ELSE CASE WHEN isnull(qryKat.KolInKat ,5000)/10>500 THEN isnull(qryKat.KolInKat ,5000)/10 ELSE 500 END END ELSE dbo.tblTehStock.kol END as TS,
--qryKat.KolInKat ,qryPok.KolPok 
--FROM dbo.tblTehStock WITH (NOLOCK) 
--LEFT OUTER JOIN
--(SELECT COUNT(*) as KolPok,SUM(dbo.tblAll.Количество ) as Kupleno, dbo.tblAll.SkladID  FROM dbo.tblShceta WITH (NOLOCK)
--INNER JOIN dbo.tblAll WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblShceta.ShcID = dbo.tblAll.ShcID 
--WHERE ДатаНакладной BETWEEN @BDta AND @EDta
--GROUP BY dbo.tblAll.SkladID) as qryPok ON dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID = qryPok.SkladID 
--LEFT OUTER JOIN
--(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
--FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
--LEFT OUTER JOIN
--(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
--WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
--GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
--GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON dbo.tblTehStock.SkladID = qryKat.SkladID) as qryTS ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
left outer join dbo.tblTehStock as qryTS WITH (NOLOCK) ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryTS.SkladID
LEFT OUTER JOIN
(SELECT SUM(Kol) as KolInKat,dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID 
FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
inner join
(SELECT MAX(NKat) as MxKat ,SkladID FROM dbo.tblNumKat WITH (NOLOCK)
WHERE dbo.tblNumKat.Used=0
GROUP BY SkladID) as q ON dbo.tblNumKat.NKat = q.MxKat AND dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID = q.SkladID 
GROUP BY dbo.tblNumKat.SkladID ) as qryKat ON qryfrmSt.Код = qryKat.SkladID 

inner join 
(
Select dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID from dbo.tblReservirSub with (nolock)
inner join dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID =  dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDRes and dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.SkladID
inner join  dbo.tblSchEl WITH (NOLOCK)  On dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.subtblRes4ReadyDate.IDSch
Where   dbo.tblSchEl.IsProblem = 1
group by dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID) as qProb on qProb.SkladID = qryfrmSt.Код 
left outer join dbo.tmpIDs with (nolock) ON qryfrmSt.Код = dbo.tmpIDs.ID and dbo.tmpIDs.Tip = 104 and dbo.tmpIDs.Who = CURRENT_USER 

WHERE
(CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) = @WhatFirm OR CAST(LEFT(qryfrmSt.WhatFirmTxt,10) as smallint) < 0)
--AND 
--(qryfrmSt.FullDolg-qryfrmSt.Недополучено + CASE WHEN qryfrmSt.Элемент='Давальческое сырье' THEN 0 ELSE ISNULL(qryTS.TS,0) END >@KolDolg )

ORDER BY qryfrmSt.Элемент, qryfrmSt.Серия, qryfrmSt.Наименование
end
drop table #TmpFrm 
drop table #TmpAvt
END

USE [TrxSQL]
GO

/****** Объект:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[xp_SubMinimumFilter]    Дата создания скрипта: 27.11.2026 13:06:05 ******/ 
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
/* источник данных для формы SubMinimumFilter (в frmSt) */
--похожая процедура xp_StatGetWarn
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_SubMinimumFilter]
(@txtID int=0,@Rgm varchar(2)='1')
AS 
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
--формируем таблицу с резервами, из-за которых элемент стал в долгах
if @Rgm='1' or @Rgm='5' or @Rgm='8'  begin
DECLARE @tmp int
DECLARE @kol int
DECLARE @IDRes int
DECLARE @t table (IDRes int)
SET @tmp = 0
if @Rgm='1' begin
	exec @tmp = dbo.xp_SumNedopolKolByElemNew @txtID,@tmp
end
SELECT @tmp = @tmp+ isnull(Количество,0) FROM dbo.Sklad WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE [Код] = @txtID
DECLARE rst CURSOR FOR
SELECT     dbo.tblReserv.Изделия * dbo.tblReservirSub.Резерв AS Кол, dbo.tblReserv.IDRes
FROM         dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) INNER JOIN
                      dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID
WHERE     (dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0) AND (dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @txtID)
ORDER BY dbo.tblReserv.Sort
OPEN rst
FETCH NEXT FROM rst INTO @kol,@IDRes
While @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 Begin
	SET @tmp=@tmp-@kol
	if @tmp<0 begin
		INSERT INTO @t (IDRes) VALUES (@IDRes)
	end
	FETCH NEXT FROM rst INTO @kol,@IDRes
END 
CLOSE rst
DEALLOCATE rst
-- до сюда формирование таблицы
SELECT IDRes,Дата,[Название темы],Firma, [Ответственный],Prim,IsExactDate,DtaPost,Изделия,Резерв, AllKol,IsGuilty,[Isp],DisagreeDate,
OtvIng,FZ275,IDProcess,NumZakas,IDRes,
case when RaznDays< 1 THEN '' ELSE cast(RaznDays / 7 as varchar(10)) + 'н.' + case when RaznDays-7*(RaznDays / 7)>0 THEN cast(RaznDays-7*(RaznDays / 7) as varchar(10)) + 'д.' ELSE '' END END as Razn,
CASE WHEN IsGuilty ='!' then ISNULL(ToxicLvlTxt,'') ELSE '' END as ToxicLvl,
CASE WHEN IsGuilty ='!' then ISNULL(INN,'') ELSE '' END as INN,
CASE WHEN IsGuilty ='!' then ISNULL(ApplyAreaTxt,'') ELSE '' END as ApplyArea,case when Trg = 0 Then '-' else cast( cast(Trg as decimal(12,4)) as varchar(10)) end as Trg, Colr,
case when Trg = 0 Then '-' else cast(case when Ddif < 4 Then cast(round(Trg*1.03,4) as decimal(12,4)) else cast(round(Trg*1.0500,4) as decimal(12,4)) end  as varchar(10)) end as MaxPrc,
PlandateCmplx,
CASE WHEN IsGuilty ='!' and Trg = 0 THEN cast(cast(PrcCalc as decimal(12,4)) as varchar(10)) ELSE '-' END as CalcPrc
FROM
(SELECT     dbo.tblReserv.IDRes, dbo.tblReserv.Дата, dbo.tblReserv.[Название темы],round(isnull(dbo.tblReservirsub.Trg ,0),4) as Trg,isnull(dbo.tblReservirsub.Colr,0) as Colr, dbo.tblFirma.Firma, 
			dbo.tblIspolnit01.FirstName as [Ответственный], dbo.tblReserv.Примечание as Prim, dbo.tblReserv.IsExactDate, ISNULL(dbo.tblReservirsub.Ddif,0) as Ddif,
			case when dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt = 0 THEN dbo.tblReserv.DtaPost ELSE isnull(dbo.tblReserv.DtaPostAvt,dbo.tblReserv.DtaPost) END as DtaPost,dbo.tblReserv.Изделия,dbo.tblReservirSub.Резерв,dbo.tblReserv.Изделия*dbo.tblReservirSub.Резерв as AllKol,
		   CASE WHEN isnull(qryR.IDRes,0)>0 THEN '!' ELSE '' END as IsGuilty,dbo.tblIsp2.[Name] as [Isp],
		   dbo.tblReserv.DisagreeDate,dbo.GetOtv4QueryKomplSQL(dbo.tblReserv.IDRes) as OtvIng,
		   CASE WHEN dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as FZ275,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDProcess,
		   dbo.tblProcess.NameProcess  as NumZakas,
		   DATEDIFF(d,cast(convert(char(8),GetDate(),112) + ' 00:00:00' as datetime),cast(convert(char(8),case when dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt = 0 THEN dbo.tblReserv.DtaPost ELSE dbo.tblReserv.DtaPostAvt END,112) + ' 00:00:00' as datetime)) as RaznDays,
		   dbo.tblReserv.Sort,dbo.qryToxicLvl.ToxicLvlTxt,dbo.qryToxicLvl.INN,dbo.qryToxicLvl.ApplyAreaTxt,
			--по ТЗ 479 Маслюкова (14.01.22)
		   case when dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt = 0 THEN qMnl.PlandateMnl ELSE isnull(qAvt.PlandateAvt,qMnl.PlandateMnl) END as PlandateCmplx,
		   qCalcPrice.Prc as PrcCalc
		   
FROM         dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) INNER JOIN
                      dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID INNER JOIN
                      dbo.tblFirma WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.Zac = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
LEFT OUTER JOIN @t as qryR ON qryR.IDRes = dbo.tblReserv.IDRes
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblIsp2 WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.WhatFirm =dbo.tblIsp2.IspID 
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblIspolnit01 WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.[Owner] = dbo.tblIspolnit01.IspolnitID 
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblProcessSum WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes 
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblProcess WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblProcessSum.IDProcess = dbo.tblProcess.ProcessID 
left outer join dbo.qryToxicLvl WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.Zac = dbo.qryToxicLvl.UchetID
--по ТЗ 412 Ивановой (26.02.21)
left outer join
(select MIN(PlanDate) as PlandateMnl,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes 
FROM dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblProcessSum with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID = dbo.tblProcessSum.IDPosProcess 
where dbo.tblSakas1.AllowOverflow = 0
GROUP BY dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes ) as qMnl ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = qMnl.IDRes
left outer join
(select MIN(PlanDate) as PlandateAvt,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes 
FROM dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblProcessSum with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID = dbo.tblProcessSum.IDPosProcess 
where dbo.tblSakas1.AllowOverflow = 1
GROUP BY dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes ) as qAvt ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = qAvt.IDRes
--
--по письму Знака от 02.02.22
left outer join
(select dbo.tblPivotDetail.Prc,qCalc.IDRes  FROM
(SELECT MAX(dbo.tblQueryKompl.ID) as MxCalc,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes FROM dbo.tblQueryKompl with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblProcessSum with (nolock) ON dbo.tblQueryKompl.IDz = dbo.tblProcessSum.NamePlaty 
WHERE  dbo.tblQueryKompl.ReqPrc =1 
and dbo.tblQueryKompl.SostMng = 1
GROUP BY dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes) qCalc
inner join dbo.tblPivotDetail with (nolock) ON qCalc.MxCalc = dbo.tblPivotDetail.IDQueryKompl and dbo.tblPivotDetail.SkladID = @txtID ) as qCalcPrice ON dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes = qCalcPrice.IDRes 

WHERE     (dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ <> 0) AND (dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0) AND (dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @txtID)

) as q1
--по ТЗ 479 Маслюкова (14.01.22)
--уточнение по письму Маслюкова от 24.01.22
ORDER BY case when IDRes = 8579 THEN 0 ELSe 1 END,IsGuilty DESC,isnull(DtaPost,'20991231') DESC,Дата DESC
end
else begin
SELECT IDRes,Дата,[Название темы],Firma, [Ответственный],Prim,IsExactDate,DtaPost,Изделия,Резерв, AllKol,'' as IsGuilty,[Isp],
OtvIng,FZ275,IDProcess,NumZakas,
case when RaznDays< 1 THEN '' ELSE cast(RaznDays / 7 as varchar(10)) + 'н.' + case when RaznDays-7*(RaznDays / 7)>0 THEN cast(RaznDays-7*(RaznDays / 7) as varchar(10)) + 'д.' ELSE '' END END as Razn,
'' as ToxicLvl,'' as INN,'' as ApplyArea,
case when Trg = 0 Then '-' else cast(case when Ddif < 4 Then cast(round(Trg*1.0300,4) as decimal(12,4)) else cast(round(Trg*1.0500,4) as decimal(12,4)) end  as varchar(10)) end as MaxPrc,
case when Trg = 0 Then '-' else cast( cast(Trg as decimal(12,4)) as varchar(10)) end as Trg,Colr,
PlandateCmplx,'-' as CalcPrc
FROM
(SELECT     dbo.tblReserv.IDRes,dbo.tblReserv.Дата, dbo.tblReserv.[Название темы], dbo.tblFirmaShort.Firma, dbo.tblIspolnit01.FirstName as [Ответственный], dbo.tblReserv.Примечание as Prim, dbo.tblReserv.IsExactDate, 
                      case when dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt = 0 THEN dbo.tblReserv.DtaPost ELSE dbo.tblReserv.DtaPostAvt END as DtaPost,dbo.tblReserv.Изделия,dbo.tblReservirSub.Резерв,dbo.tblReserv.Изделия*dbo.tblReservirSub.Резерв as AllKol,
[Name] as [Isp],dbo.GetOtv4QueryKomplSQL(dbo.tblReserv.IDRes) as OtvIng,
round(isnull(dbo.tblReservirsub.Trg ,0),4) as Trg,isnull(dbo.tblReservirsub.Colr,0) as Colr,ISNULL(dbo.tblReservirsub.Ddif,0) as Ddif,
CASE WHEN dbo.tblReserv.FZ275ID >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as FZ275,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDProcess ,dbo.tblProcess.NameProcess as NumZakas,DATEDIFF(d,cast(convert(char(8),GetDate(),112) + ' 00:00:00' as datetime),cast(convert(char(8),case when dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt = 0 THEN dbo.tblReserv.DtaPost ELSE dbo.tblReserv.DtaPostAvt END,112) + ' 00:00:00' as datetime)) as RaznDays,
dbo.tblReserv.Sort,
--по ТЗ 479 Маслюкова (14.01.22)
case when dbo.tblReservirSub.Avt = 0 THEN qMnl.PlandateMnl ELSE isnull(qAvt.PlandateAvt,qMnl.PlandateMnl) END as PlandateCmplx
FROM         dbo.tblReserv WITH (NOLOCK) INNER JOIN
                      dbo.tblReservirSub WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = dbo.tblReservirSub.ResID INNER JOIN
                      dbo.tblFirmaShort WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.Zac = dbo.tblFirmaShort.UchetID
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblIsp2 WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.WhatFirm =dbo.tblIsp2.IspID 
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblIspolnit01 WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.[Owner] = dbo.tblIspolnit01.IspolnitID 
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblProcessSum WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes 
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.tblProcess WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblProcessSum.IDProcess = dbo.tblProcess.ProcessID 
--по ТЗ 412 Ивановой (26.02.21)
left outer join
(select MIN(PlanDate) as PlandateMnl,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes 
FROM dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblProcessSum with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID = dbo.tblProcessSum.IDPosProcess 
where dbo.tblSakas1.AllowOverflow = 0
GROUP BY dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes ) as qMnl ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = qMnl.IDRes
left outer join
(select MIN(PlanDate) as PlandateAvt,dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes 
FROM dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblProcessSum with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID = dbo.tblProcessSum.IDPosProcess 
where dbo.tblSakas1.AllowOverflow = 1
GROUP BY dbo.tblProcessSum.IDRes ) as qAvt ON dbo.tblReserv.IDRes = qAvt.IDRes
--
WHERE     (dbo.tblReservirSub.Activ <> 0) AND (dbo.tblReserv.Riba = 0) AND (dbo.tblReservirSub.SkladID = @txtID)
) as q1
--по ТЗ 479 Маслюкова (14.01.22)
--уточнение по письму Маслюкова от 24.01.22
ORDER BY case when IDRes = 8579 THEN 0 ELSe 1 END,IsGuilty DESC,isnull(DtaPost,'20991231') DESC,Дата DESC
end
END

/****** Объект:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[xp_SkladTrackingZakLst]    Дата создания скрипта: 27.11.2026 13:06:33 ******/ 
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	<Description,,>
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_SkladTrackingZakLst] 
(@SkladID int=0)
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
SELECT case when dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking.IDProcess>0 then 'да' ELSE 'нет' end as [Ознакомлен],
qSel.NameProcess as [Заказ],qSel.[Ближ.старт],Kol as [Кол.изд],dbo.tblZadan.NameFile as [Название]
FROM
(select  
dbo.tblProcess.NameProcess,convert(char(10),min(dbo.tblGraficRabot.BegPlan),104) as [Ближ.старт]
,MAX(dbo.tblPosProcess.KolvoToZavod) as Kol,
dbo.tblProcess.ProcessID,dbo.subtblRecomMat.IDz
from dbo.subtblRecomMat with (nolock)
inner join dbo.tblPosProcess with (nolock) ON dbo.subtblRecomMat.IDz = dbo.tblPosProcess.NamePlaty
inner join dbo.tblProcess with (nolock) ON dbo.tblProcess.ProcessID = dbo.tblPosProcess.IDProcess
inner join dbo.tblSakas1 with (nolock) ON dbo.tblPosProcess.ID = dbo.tblSakas1.PosProcessID
inner join dbo.tblArb with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSakas1.SakasID = dbo.tblArb.IDSakas1
inner join dbo.tblGraficRabot with (nolock) ON dbo.tblArb.IDArb = dbo.tblGraficRabot.WhatWork
WHERE dbo.tblSakas1.SostSak < 4 and dbo.tblGraficRabot.BegPlan > 0
and dbo.subtblRecomMat.SkladID = @SkladID
GROUP BY dbo.tblProcess.NameProcess,dbo.tblProcess.ProcessID,dbo.subtblRecomMat.IDz) as qSel
inner join dbo.tblZadan with (nolock) ON qSel.IDz = dbo.tblZadan.IDZadan
left outer join dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking with (nolock) ON qSel.ProcessID =dbo.SubtblProcess4Tracking.IDProcess
order by [Ближ.старт]
END

/****** Объект:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[xp_GetInvoices1]    Дата создания скрипта: 27.11.2026 13:06:58 ******/ 
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- =============================================
-- Author:		<Author,,Name>
-- Create date: <Create Date,,>
-- Description:	список предложений от поставщиков по элементу
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[xp_GetInvoices1]		
(@SkladID int=0,@Numtbl int=0,@IDRes int=0,@Tpe int = 0,@DtaTxt varchar(16)='')
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;

update dbo.tblInvoice set PrcRub = dbo.Txt2HourSQL(isnull([hrs],'0:0'))
*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100.00*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour,Prc = dbo.Txt2HourSQL(isnull([hrs],'0:0'))
*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100.00*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour/dbo.GetKursSQL(convert(varchar(8),dbo.tblInvoice.DT,112),840)
from dbo.tblInvoice 
inner join  dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper
inner join dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
where oper = 'Вид обработки' and skladid = @SkladID


if @SkladID >0 begin
	if @IDRes= 0 begin
		if @Numtbl>=0 begin
			DECLARE @BDta datetime
			DECLARE @EDta datetime
			if @DtaTxt = '' begin
				SELECT @BDta = min(DtaAdv),@EDta = max(DtaAdv) FROM dbo.tblInvoice with (nolock) WHERE SkladID = @SkladID
			end
			else begin	
				SET @BDta = cast(left(@DTaTxt,8) as datetime)
				SET @EDta = cast(right(@DTaTxt,8) + ' 23:59:59' as datetime)
			end
			--dbo.tblFirma.Firma по ТЗ 587 Знака (19.09.23)
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.Firma as Post,DtaAdv,dbo.tblInvoice.Srok,Prc,dbo.tblInvoice.Prim,ID,Kolvo,dbo.tblInvoice.UserCur,dbo.tblInvoice.DT,
			case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
PrcRub,dbo.tblInvoice.email,PrcE,Analog,AnalogActive,
			CASE WHEN NumPivot>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as InPivot,
			Numtbl,CASE WHEN Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as IsCurTbl,
			MntAnalog,ProjectPrice,NmeCat,CodeCat,
			
			PrcWONDS,dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID ,dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID,
			dbo.RateMeSQL(dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID) as RateTxt,
			dbo.ExistCatSQL(dbo.tblFirma.Firma) as ExistCat,
			dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.txt as SostAgreed,MOQ,MPQ,DC,PrcEWONDS,PriceUWONDS,
			isnull(Prc,0) * Kolvo as TotalUSD,PrcRub * Kolvo as TotalRUR,PostID,
			ISNULL(qSch.NSch,'') as NSchByInvoice,
			--по заданию Реннике от 03.08.21
			CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Prc = 0 THEN 0 ELSE floor(50 / dbo.tblInvoice.Prc) + Kolvo end as MaxKol,
			dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed,
			--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (12.09.23)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcY,
			--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (02.02.24)
			dbo.tblSklad.ЦенаОптовая as PrcOpt,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 2 Маслюкова (24.09.24)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcWOKfs, dbo.tblInvoice.PrcSrc,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 4 (11.02.26)
			dbo.tblInvoice.Manuf,dbo.tblInvoice.oper,dbo.tblInvoice.suboper,dbo.tblInvoice.hrs,dbo.tblInvoice.absid,
				case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then dbo.Txt2HourSQL([hrs])
*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
			--письмо Знака от 28.09.26
			dbo.tblSprVl.Nme as TypeVl,
			--заявка Знака от 12.10.26
			dbo.tblInvoice.SrcLT
			FROM dbo.tblInvoice WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join dbo.tblSklad with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID = dbo.tblSklad.Код
			inner join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID 
			inner join dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed = dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.WhatDiv and dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.Tip = 1
			left outer join
			(select dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice,dbo.tblPredShceta.НомерСчета + ' от ' + CONVERT(char(8),dbo.tblPredShceta.ДатаСчета ,4) as NSch  from dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
			inner join dbo.tblPredShceta with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.tblPredShceta.PredShcID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice >0) as qSch ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = qSch.IDInvoice 
			left outer join dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper  and IsShown = 1
			left outer join  dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
			left outer join dbo.tblSprVl with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.NmeVl = dbo.tblSprVl.ByBank
			WHERE SkladID = @SkladID and Tpe = @Tpe
			and dbo.tblInvoice.DtaAdv between @BDta and @EDta 
			ORDER BY CASE WHEN ProjectPrice>0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, CASE WHEN Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END DESC,DtaAdv DESC
			RETURN
		end
		else begin
			--dbo.tblFirma.Firma по ТЗ 587 Знака (19.09.23)
			SELECT TOP 10 dbo.tblFirma.Firma as Post,DtaAdv,dbo.tblInvoice.Srok,Prc,dbo.tblInvoice.Prim,ID,Kolvo,UserCur,DT,PrcRub,dbo.tblInvoice.email,PrcE,Analog,AnalogActive,
			CASE WHEN NumPivot>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as InPivot,
			Numtbl,CASE WHEN Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as IsCurTbl,
			MntAnalog,ProjectPrice,NmeCat,CodeCat,PrcWONDS,dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID,dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID,
			dbo.RateMeSQL(dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID) as RateTxt,dbo.ExistCatSQL(dbo.tblFirma.Firma) as ExistCat,
			dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.txt as SostAgreed,MOQ,MPQ,DC,PrcEWONDS,PriceUWONDS,isnull(Prc,0) * Kolvo as TotalUSD,isnull(PrcRub,0) * Kolvo as TotalRUR,PostID,
			--по заданию Реннике от 03.08.21
			CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Prc = 0 THEN 0 ELSE floor(50 / Prc) + Kolvo end as MaxKol,
			dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed,
			--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (12.09.23)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcY,
			Null as PrcOpt,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 2 Маслюкова (24.09.24)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcWOKfs, dbo.tblInvoice.PrcSrc,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 4 (11.02.26)
			dbo.tblInvoice.Manuf,dbo.tblInvoice.oper,dbo.tblInvoice.suboper,dbo.tblInvoice.hrs,dbo.tblInvoice.absid,	case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
			--письмо Знака от 28.09.26
			dbo.tblSprVl.Nme as TypeVl,
			--заявка Знака от 12.10.26
			dbo.tblInvoice.SrcLT
			FROM dbo.tblInvoice WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
			inner join dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed = dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.WhatDiv and dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.Tip = 1
						left outer join dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper  and IsShown = 1
			left outer join  dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
			left outer join dbo.tblSprVl with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.NmeVl = dbo.tblSprVl.ByBank
			WHERE SkladID = @SkladID and Tpe = @Tpe
			ORDER BY CASE WHEN ProjectPrice>0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,DtaAdv DESC
			RETURN
		end
	end
	else begin
		if @IDRes > 0 begin
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.Firma as Post,DtaAdv,dbo.tblInvoice.Srok,dbo.tblInvoice.Prc,dbo.tblInvoice.Prim,ID,Kolvo,UserCur,DT,PrcRub,dbo.tblInvoice.email,PrcE,Analog,AnalogActive,
			CASE WHEN NumPivot>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as InPivot,
			dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl,CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as IsCurTbl,
			MntAnalog ,ProjectPrice,NmeCat,CodeCat,PrcWONDS,dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID,dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID,
			dbo.RateMeSQL(dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID) as RateTxt,dbo.ExistCatSQL(dbo.tblFirma.Firma) as ExistCat,
			dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.txt as SostAgreed,MOQ,MPQ,DC,PrcEWONDS,PriceUWONDS,isnull(PrcRub  ,0) * Kolvo as TotalRUR,PostID,
			ISNULL(qSch.NSch,'') as NSchByInvoice,
			--по заданию Реннике от 03.08.21
			CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Prc = 0 THEN 0 ELSE floor(50 / dbo.tblInvoice.Prc) + Kolvo end as MaxKol,
			dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed,
			--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (12.09.23)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcY,
			Null as PrcOpt,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 2 Маслюкова (24.09.24)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcWOKfs, dbo.tblInvoice.PrcSrc,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 4 (11.02.26)
			dbo.tblInvoice.Manuf,dbo.tblInvoice.oper,dbo.tblInvoice.suboper,dbo.tblInvoice.hrs,dbo.tblInvoice.absid,
				case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then dbo.Txt2HourSQL([hrs])
*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
			--письмо Знака от 28.09.26
			dbo.tblSprVl.Nme as TypeVl,
			--заявка Знака от 12.10.26
			dbo.tblInvoice.SrcLT
			FROM dbo.tblInvoice WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
			INNER JOIN dbo.tblPivotDetail WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = dbo.tblPivotDetail.IDInv
			inner join dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed = dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.WhatDiv and dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.Tip = 1
			left outer join
			(select dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice,dbo.tblPredShceta.НомерСчета + ' от ' + CONVERT(char(8),dbo.tblPredShceta.ДатаСчета ,4) as NSch  from dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
			inner join dbo.tblPredShceta with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.tblPredShceta.PredShcID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice >0) as qSch ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = qSch.IDInvoice
									left outer join dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper
			left outer join  dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
			left outer join dbo.tblSprVl with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.NmeVl = dbo.tblSprVl.ByBank
			WHERE dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID = @SkladID and Tpe = @Tpe
			AND dbo.tblPivotDetail.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND dbo.tblPivotDetail.IDRes = @IDRes 
			ORDER BY CASE WHEN ProjectPrice>0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END DESC,DtaAdv DESC
			RETURN
		end
		if @IDRes =-1 begin
			SELECT dbo.tblFirma.Firma as Post,DtaAdv,dbo.tblInvoice.Srok,Prc,dbo.tblInvoice.Prim,ID,Kolvo,UserCur,DT,PrcRub,dbo.tblInvoice.email,PrcE,Analog,AnalogActive,
			CASE WHEN NumPivot>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as InPivot,
			Numtbl,CASE WHEN Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as IsCurTbl,
			MntAnalog,ProjectPrice,NmeCat,CodeCat,PrcWONDS,SkladID,dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID,
			dbo.RateMeSQL(dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID) as RateTxt,dbo.ExistCatSQL(dbo.tblFirma.Firma) as ExistCat,
			dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.txt as SostAgreed,MOQ,MPQ,DC,PrcEWONDS,PriceUWONDS,isnull(Prc,0) * Kolvo as TotalUSD,isnull(PrcRub,0) * Kolvo as TotalRUR,PostID,
			ISNULL(qSch.NSch,'') as NSchByInvoice,
			--по заданию Реннике от 03.08.21
			CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Prc = 0 THEN 0 ELSE floor(50 / dbo.tblInvoice.Prc) + Kolvo end as MaxKol,
			dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed,
			--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (12.09.23)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcY,
			Null as PrcOpt,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 2 Маслюкова (24.09.24)
			dbo.tblInvoice.PrcWOKfs, dbo.tblInvoice.PrcSrc,
			--ТЗ 348 итерация 4 (11.02.26)
			dbo.tblInvoice.Manuf,dbo.tblInvoice.oper,dbo.tblInvoice.suboper,dbo.tblInvoice.hrs,dbo.tblInvoice.absid,	case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
			--письмо Знака от 28.09.26
			dbo.tblSprVl.Nme as TypeVl,
			--заявка Знака от 12.10.26
			dbo.tblInvoice.SrcLT
			FROM dbo.tblInvoice WITH (NOLOCK)
			inner join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
			inner join dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed = dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.WhatDiv and dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.Tip = 1
			left outer join
			(select dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice,dbo.tblPredShceta.НомерСчета + ' от ' + CONVERT(char(8),dbo.tblPredShceta.ДатаСчета ,4) as NSch  from dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
			inner join dbo.tblPredShceta with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.tblPredShceta.PredShcID 
			WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice >0) as qSch ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = qSch.IDInvoice
												left outer join dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper
			left outer join  dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
			left outer join dbo.tblSprVl with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.NmeVl = dbo.tblSprVl.ByBank
			WHERE SkladID = @SkladID AND Numtbl = @Numtbl and Tpe = @Tpe
			ORDER BY CASE WHEN ProjectPrice>0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, CASE WHEN Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END DESC,DtaAdv DESC
			RETURN
		end
	end
end
if @SkladID <0 begin
	Declare @CopyNum int
	SET @CopyNum =-1
	DECLARE @TmpEl TABLE (SkladID int PRIMARY KEY)
	DECLARE @Naim nvarchar(500)
	SET @Naim = ''
	SELECT @CopyNum = CopyNum FROM dbo.tblPivotMainLog WHERE Num = @Numtbl 
	SELECT @Naim = Naim FROM dbo.tblPivotDetail WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE Numtbl = @Numtbl 
	AND SkladID = @SkladID 
	
	DECLARE @TmpCopy TABLE (id int identity,NumCopy int PRIMARY KEY)
	INSERT INTO @TmpCopy (NumCopy ) Values (@Numtbl)
	If @CopyNum > 0 Begin
	While @CopyNum>0 begin
	INSERT INTO @TmpCopy (NumCopy ) Values (@CopyNum)
	Set @CopyNum=0
	SELECT @CopyNum = isnull(CopyNum,0) FROM dbo.tblPivotMainLog WHERE Num = (SELECT TOP 1 NumCopy From @TmpCopy Order BY Id desc)
	end
	End
	if @Naim <> '' begin
		INSERT INTO @TmpEl (SkladID )
		SELECT SkladID FROM dbo.tblPivotDetail WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE SkladID <0 AND Naim = @Naim
		AND Numtbl in (SELECT NumCopy  FROM @TmpCopy)
		GROUP BY SkladID
	end
	else begin
		INSERT INTO @TmpEl (SkladID )
		VALUES (@SkladID )
	end
	if @IDRes= 0 begin
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.Firma as Post,DtaAdv,dbo.tblInvoice.Srok,dbo.tblInvoice.Prc,dbo.tblInvoice.Prim,dbo.tblInvoice.ID,Kolvo,UserCur,DT,PrcRub,dbo.tblInvoice.email,PrcE,Analog,AnalogActive,
		CASE WHEN NumPivot>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as InPivot,dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl,CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as IsCurTbl,
		MntAnalog,ProjectPrice,NmeCat,CodeCat,PrcWONDS,dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID,dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID,
		dbo.RateMeSQL(dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID) as RateTxt,dbo.ExistCatSQL(dbo.tblFirma.Firma) as ExistCat,
		dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.txt as SostAgreed,MOQ,MPQ,DC,PrcEWONDS,PriceUWONDS,isnull(Prc,0) * Kolvo as TotalUSD,isnull( PrcRub ,0) * Kolvo as TotalRUR,PostID,
		ISNULL(qSch.NSch,'') as NSchByInvoice,
		--по заданию Реннике от 03.08.21
		CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Prc = 0 THEN 0 ELSE floor(50 / dbo.tblInvoice.Prc) + Kolvo end as MaxKol,
		dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed,
		--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (12.09.23)
		dbo.tblInvoice.PrcY,
		Null as PrcOpt,
		--ТЗ 348 итерация 2 Маслюкова (24.09.24)
		dbo.tblInvoice.PrcWOKfs, dbo.tblInvoice.PrcSrc,
		--ТЗ 348 итерация 4 (11.02.26)
		dbo.tblInvoice.Manuf,dbo.tblInvoice.oper,dbo.tblInvoice.suboper,dbo.tblInvoice.hrs,dbo.tblInvoice.absid,	case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then 
dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
		--письмо Знака от 28.09.26
		dbo.tblSprVl.Nme as TypeVl,
		--заявка Знака от 12.10.26
		dbo.tblInvoice.SrcLT
		FROM dbo.tblInvoice WITH (NOLOCK)
		inner join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
		--INNER JOIN dbo.tblPivotDetail WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = dbo.tblPivotDetail.IDInv
		inner join dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed = dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.WhatDiv and dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.Tip = 1
		INNER JOIN 
		(SELECT Naim, dbo.tblPivotDetail.SkladID,Numtbl FROM dbo.tblPivotDetail WITH (NOLOCK)
		INNER JOIN @TmpEl as q1 ON dbo.tblPivotDetail.SkladID = q1.SkladID
		inner join @TmpCopy as q2 On q2.NumCopy = dbo.tblPivotDetail.Numtbl
		group by Naim, dbo.tblPivotDetail.SkladID,Numtbl
		) as qryNaim
		ON dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID = qryNaim.SkladID AND dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl  = qryNaim.Numtbl 
		INNER JOIN @TmpEl as q ON dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID = q.SkladID 
		inner join @TmpCopy as q2 On q2.NumCopy = dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl
		AND qryNaim.Naim = @Naim 
		left outer join
		(select dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice,dbo.tblPredShceta.НомерСчета + ' от ' + CONVERT(char(8),dbo.tblPredShceta.ДатаСчета ,4) as NSch  from dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblPredShceta with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.tblPredShceta.PredShcID 
		WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice >0) as qSch ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = qSch.IDInvoice
														left outer join dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper
			left outer join  dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
			left outer join dbo.tblSprVl with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.NmeVl = dbo.tblSprVl.ByBank
		where Tpe = @Tpe
		ORDER BY CASE WHEN ProjectPrice>0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END DESC, DtaAdv DESC
		RETURN
	end
	else begin
		SELECT dbo.tblFirma.Firma as Post,DtaAdv,dbo.tblInvoice.Srok,dbo.tblInvoice.Prc,dbo.tblInvoice.Prim,dbo.tblInvoice.ID,Kolvo,UserCur,DT,PrcRub,dbo.tblInvoice.email,PrcE,Analog,AnalogActive,
		CASE WHEN NumPivot>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as InPivot,dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl,CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END as IsCurTbl,
		MntAnalog,ProjectPrice,NmeCat,CodeCat,PrcWONDS,dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID,dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID,
		dbo.RateMeSQL(dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID) as RateTxt,dbo.ExistCatSQL(dbo.tblFirma.Firma) as ExistCat,
		dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.txt as SostAgreed,MOQ,MPQ,DC,PrcEWONDS,PriceUWONDS,isnull(dbo.tblInvoice.Prc,0) * Kolvo as TotalUSD,isnull(PrcRub,0) * Kolvo as TotalRUR,PostID,
		ISNULL(qSch.NSch,'') as NSchByInvoice,
		--по заданию Реннике от 03.08.21
		CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Prc = 0 THEN 0 ELSE floor(50 / dbo.tblInvoice.Prc) + Kolvo end as MaxKol,
		dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed,
		--ТЗ 592 Маслюкова (12.09.23)
		dbo.tblInvoice.PrcY,
		Null as PrcOpt,
		--ТЗ 348 итерация 2 Маслюкова (24.09.24)
		dbo.tblInvoice.PrcWOKfs, dbo.tblInvoice.PrcSrc,
		--ТЗ 348 итерация 4 (11.02.26)
		dbo.tblInvoice.Manuf,dbo.tblInvoice.oper,dbo.tblInvoice.suboper,dbo.tblInvoice.hrs,dbo.tblInvoice.absid,	case when dbo.tblInvoice.oper = 'Вид обработки' Then 
dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NlgKfs/100*dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.SmPerHour else 1 end as hrsprc,
		--письмо Знака от 28.09.26
		dbo.tblSprVl.Nme as TypeVl,
		--заявка Знака от 12.10.26
		dbo.tblInvoice.SrcLT
		FROM dbo.tblInvoice WITH (NOLOCK)
		inner join dbo.tblFirma with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.UchetID = dbo.tblFirma.UchetID
		INNER JOIN dbo.tblPivotDetail WITH (NOLOCK) ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = dbo.tblPivotDetail.IDInv
		inner join dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.IsAgreed = dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.WhatDiv and dbo.tblInvoiceAgreedSpr.Tip = 1
		INNER JOIN @TmpEl as q ON dbo.tblInvoice.SkladID = q.SkladID 
		inner join @TmpCopy as q2 On q2.NumCopy = dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl
		AND dbo.tblPivotDetail.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND dbo.tblPivotDetail.IDRes = @IDRes
		left outer join
		(select dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice,dbo.tblPredShceta.НомерСчета + ' от ' + CONVERT(char(8),dbo.tblPredShceta.ДатаСчета ,4) as NSch  from dbo.tblSchEl with (nolock)
		inner join dbo.tblPredShceta with (nolock) ON dbo.tblSchEl.ShcID = dbo.tblPredShceta.PredShcID 
		WHERE dbo.tblSchEl.IDInvoice >0) as qSch ON dbo.tblInvoice.ID = qSch.IDInvoice
														left outer join dbo.tblSubTypeArb with (nolock) On dbo.tblSubTypeArb.TypeArb = dbo.tblInvoice.suboper
			left outer join  dbo.tblSprGroupWrks  with (nolock) On dbo.tblSprGroupWrks.NumWork = dbo.tblSubTypeArb.NumTypeArb
		left outer join dbo.tblSprVl with (nolock) ON dbo.tblInvoice.NmeVl = dbo.tblSprVl.ByBank
				where Tpe = @Tpe
		ORDER BY CASE WHEN ProjectPrice>0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,CASE WHEN dbo.tblInvoice.Numtbl = @Numtbl AND @NumTbl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END DESC, DtaAdv DESC
		RETURN
	end
end
END
